  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "An elliptic capacity test uses the same shape and orientation in the two spatial coordinates, with independently chosen centers. The terminal test keeps the $U$-ellipse and contracts the position ellipse by $M^{−1}$. The picture depicts testing sets, not a product assumption on the law; the displayed contraction is schematic.",
      "figure_id": "fig:capacity-tests",
      "pdf_page": 10,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:capacity-tests",
          "exact_quote": "An elliptic capacity test uses the same shape and orientation\nin the two spatial coordinates, with independently chosen centers.\nThe terminal test keeps the \\(U\\)-ellipse and contracts the\nposition ellipse by \\(M^{-1}\\). The picture depicts",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1cm,y=1cm,>=Stealth,\n  axes/.style={gray!65,thin},\n  capacityellipse/.style={draw=linkblue,fill=linkblue!8,thick}]\n  \\foreach \\x/\\name in {0/U,4.2/V,8.4/X_v} {\n    \\begin{scope}[shift={(\\x,0)}]\n      \\draw[axes,->] (-1.25,0)--(1.35,0);\n      \\draw[axes,->] (0,-.85)--(0,1.15);\n      \\node at (0,-1.15) {\\(\\name\\)-plane};\n    \\end{scope}\n  }\n  \\begin{scope}[shift={(.12,.20)},rotate=25]\n    \\draw[capacityellipse] (0,0) ellipse (1.00 and .37);\n    \\draw[linkblue,dashed] (-1,0)--(1,0);\n  \\end{scope}\n  \\begin{scope}[shift={(4.05,.26)},rotate=25]\n    \\draw[capacityellipse] (0,0) ellipse (1.00 and .37);\n    \\draw[linkblue,dashed] (-1,0)--(1,0);\n  \\end{scope}\n  \\begin{scope}[shift={(8.57,.21)},rotate=25]\n    \\draw[capacityellipse] (0,0) ellipse (.50 and .185);\n    \\draw[linkblue,dashed] (-.5,0)--(.5,0);\n  \\end{scope}\n  \\node at (.05,1.4) {\\(E+u_0\\)};\n  \\node at (4.15,1.4) {\\(E+v_0\\)};\n  \\node at (8.45,1.4) {\\(M^{-1}E+x_0\\)};\n  \\draw[decorate,decoration={brace,mirror,amplitude=4pt}]\n    (-1.1,-1.53)--(5.3,-1.53);\n  \\node at (2.1,-1.98) {initial test};\n  \\draw[->,thick] (5.8,.3)--(6.8,.3)\n    node[midway,above] {\\(V+vU\\)};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Three tilted ellipses arranged horizontally:\n- Left ellipse: labeled \"$E + u_0$\" above, with an interior diagonal arrow along the major axis pointing up and right, labeled \"$U$-plane\" below.\n- Middle ellipse: labeled \"$E + v_0$\" above, with an interior diagonal arrow along the major axis pointing up and right, labeled \"$V$-plane\" below.\n- A horizontal brace beneath the $U$-plane and $V$-plane is labeled \"initial test\".\n- A horizontal arrow pointing right from the $V$-plane to the $X_v$-plane is labeled \"$V + vU$\" above.\n- Right ellipse: labeled \"$M^{-1}E + x_0$\" above, shown as a smaller contracted ellipse with an interior diagonal arrow along the major axis pointing up and right, labeled \"$X_v$-plane\" below."
    },
    {
      "caption": "The two extrema in the packet proof. Here $s$ is the infimum of the terminal time-support exponents permitting amplification approaching $γ$. Graph paths run from initial packet indices (roots) to terminal indices (leaves). Minimizing the limiting exponent of the path count per original root forces later coordinate selections to retain enough paths whenever they preserve nearly maximal amplification. Uniform counting on a selected path set supplies the time law needed for geometric propagation. The second- and fourth-moment estimates concern the exact masked coefficient maps.",
      "figure_id": "fig:packet-proof-map",
      "pdf_page": 84,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "fig:packet-proof-map",
          "exact_quote": "The two extrema in the packet proof. Here \\(s\\) is the infimum\nof the terminal time-support exponents permitting amplification approaching\n\\(\\gamma\\). Graph paths run from initial packet indices (roots) to\nterminal indices (leaves). Minimiz",
          "source_object": "fig:0002"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[\n  >=Stealth,\n  proofbox/.style={draw=linkblue!70,fill=linkblue!4,rounded corners=2pt,\n    align=center,text width=4.6cm,minimum height=1.05cm,\n    inner sep=5pt,font=\\small},\n  proofarrow/.style={->,thick,draw=linkblue!80}]\n  \\node[proofbox] (time) at (-3.15,0)\n    {First extremum: \\(s\\)\\\\\\(\\gamma_\\sigma<\\gamma\\) for \\(\\sigma<s\\)};\n  \\node[proofbox] (paths) at (3.15,0)\n    {Second extremum: paths per root\\\\retain paths under later masks};\n  \\node[proofbox] (law) at (-3.15,-1.75)\n    {Control conditional time-bin\\\\counts and probabilities};\n  \\node[proofbox] (leaves) at (3.15,-1.75)\n    {Select terminal coefficients\\\\control incoming path counts};\n  \\node[proofbox] (capacity) at (-3.15,-3.5)\n    {Geometric propagation\\\\bound output capacity};\n  \\node[proofbox] (mass) at (3.15,-3.5)\n    {Exact maps and refined decoupling\\\\second and fourth moments};\n  \\node[proofbox,text width=5.2cm] (end) at (0,-5.3)\n    {Capacity--mass cancellation\\\\\\(\\gamma\\le10\\kappa\\)};\n  \\draw[proofarrow] (time)--(paths);\n  \\draw[proofarrow] (paths)--(leaves);\n  \\draw[proofarrow] (leaves)--(law);\n  \\draw[proofarrow] (law)--(capacity);\n  \\draw[proofarrow] (leaves)--(mass);\n  \\draw[proofarrow] (capacity.south)--(end.north west);\n  \\draw[proofarrow] (mass.south)--(end.north east);\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A flow diagram consisting of seven rectangular boxes connected by arrows:\n- Top-left box: contains \"First extremum: $s$\" and \"$\\gamma_\\sigma < \\gamma$ for $\\sigma < s$\". A horizontal arrow points from its right edge to the top-right box, and a vertical arrow points downwards to the middle-left box.\n- Top-right box: contains \"Second extremum:\" / \"paths per root\" / \"retain paths un-\" / \"der later masks\". A vertical arrow points downwards to the middle-right box.\n- Middle-left box: contains \"Control conditional time-bin\" / \"counts and probabilities\". A horizontal arrow points from its right edge to the middle-right box, and a vertical arrow points downwards to the lower-left box.\n- Middle-right box: contains \"Select terminal coefficients\" / \"control incoming path counts\". A vertical arrow points downwards to the lower-right box.\n- Lower-left box: contains \"Geometric propagation\" / \"bound output capacity\". A downward-right diagonal arrow points to the bottom box.\n- Lower-right box: contains \"Exact maps and re-\" / \"fined decoupling\" / \"second and fourth moments\". A downward-left diagonal arrow points to the bottom box.\n- Bottom box: contains \"Capacity–mass cancellation\" and \"$\\gamma \\le 10\\kappa$\"."
    },
    {
      "caption": "Schematic localization neighborhoods in one position coordinate. At the marked child time bin, the intermediate neighborhood centered at the selected low child $b$ contains every low child of the parent in that bin and is contained in the parent’s high neighborhood.
